### [1] [Adversarially Guided Diffusion for LiDAR Range Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09787)
*Stavros Bouras,Antonios Makris,Alexandros Gkillas,Aris S. Lalos,Konstantinos Tserpes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对2D范围图像LiDAR语义分割的扩散模型无限制对抗攻击方法，通过在采样过程中直接应用分割损失引导，在保持数据流形接近的同时诱导结构化分割错误，实现了可控的攻击效果和真实感之间的良好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR语义分割的误预测会影响下游规划和安全关键决策；虽然对抗攻击在图像分类和3D点云分割中已有广泛研究，但针对2D范围图像（3D点云投影）的无限制对抗示例研究仍很少。

Method: 提出首个基于扩散模型的2D范围图像无限制对抗攻击方法，通过在采样过程中直接应用分割损失进行对抗引导，生成保持在学习到的LiDAR数据流形附近的对抗示例。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI数据集上使用RangeNet++和CENet分割网络进行实验，该方法在引导强度下可调整攻击效果，并在分割架构间实现迁移攻击。与范数有界FGSM和SegPGD基线相比，该攻击在保持竞争性的分布和视觉真实感的同时，提供了独特的有效性-真实感权衡。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持对抗示例真实感的同时，实现了可控的白盒和迁移攻击退化效果，为LiDAR语义分割系统的安全性评估提供了重要工具。

Abstract: LiDAR semantic segmentation is a key perception task in autonomous driving, where false predictions can affect downstream planning and safety-critical decision-making. Although adversarial attacks, and specifically adversarial examples, have been widely studied for image classification and 3D point cloud segmentation, unrestricted adversarial examples remain largely unexplored in the space of 2D range images, which are projections of 3D point clouds. The proposed method is, to the best of our knowledge, the first diffusion-based unrestricted adversarial attack against 2D range-image segmentation, using adversarial guidance from a segmentation loss. By applying guidance directly during sampling, the method produces unrestricted adversarial examples that remain close to the learned LiDAR data manifold while inducing structured segmentation errors. Experiments on the SemanticKITTI dataset using RangeNet++ and CENet segmentation networks demonstrate that the attack provides adjustable degradation across guidance strengths and transfers across segmentation architectures. Compared with norm-bounded FGSM and SegPGD baselines, the proposed attack offers a distinct effectiveness-realism trade-off, achieving controllable white-box and transfer degradation while maintaining competitive distributional and visual realism.

</details>


### [2] [FlashBEV: Fast and Memory-Efficient Exact BEV Transformation with IO-Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10071)
*Shunsuke Yokokawa,Hironori Kasahara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlashBEV提出了一种高效执行策略，通过重新设计采样视图转换（Sampling-VT）的执行方式，将内存占用从O(H×C×H×W)降低到O(BCXY)，实现了比传统Tensorized Sampling-VT高出一个数量级的内存减少和显著的推理延迟加速，使高分辨率长距离BEV感知在内存受限设备上成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样视图转换（Sampling-VT）支持密集连续的BEV聚合，但其标准张量化实现需要显式生成高度相关的中间张量，导致内存和延迟成本随垂直分辨率和相机数量线性增长，成为部署瓶颈。

Method: 从操作执行角度重新审视Tensorized Sampling-VT，发现其遵循gather-reduction模式，每个BEV查询独立跨相机和高度bin累加贡献，通过线程局部累加和实时重计算消除了对高度和相机依赖中间结果的显式存储，提出FlashBEV——一种完全融合且IO感知的执行策略，在数学上等价于Tensorized Sampling-VT但大幅减少全局内存流量和内核启动开销。

Result: FlashBEV实现比传统方法高出一个数量级的峰值GPU内存减少（内存与高度bin数量无关），显著加速推理延迟，峰值内存降至仅输出大小O(BCXY)，在固定预算下解锁更高范围/分辨率和垂直离散化的BEV感知能力。

Conclusion: FlashBEV通过执行重设计（数学等价但执行不同）移除了采样视图转换部署的关键可扩展性障碍，为内存受限设备上的高效、高分辨率BEV感知提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Bird's-eye-view (BEV) perception is a core component of camera-based 3D understanding in autonomous driving, where view transformation (VT) maps multi-camera image features into a unified BEV representation. Sampling-based view transformation (Sampling-VT) is attractive because it supports dense and continuous BEV aggregation for high-resolution and long-range perception. Its deployment bottleneck, however, is systems-level: standard tensorized implementations of Sampling-VT -- which we refer to as Tensorized Sampling-VT -- explicitly materialize large height-dependent intermediate tensors, causing memory and latency costs that scale poorly with vertical resolution and the number of cameras. We revisit Tensorized Sampling-VT from an operator-execution perspective and show that it follows a gather-reduction pattern: each BEV query independently accumulates contributions across cameras and height bins, enabling thread-local accumulation with on-the-fly recomputation that eliminates the need to materialize height- and camera-dependent intermediates. Based on this insight, we propose FlashBEV, a fully fused and IO-aware execution strategy mathematically equivalent to Tensorized Sampling-VT (same operator output) while substantially reducing global memory traffic and kernel-launch overhead. Experiments show that FlashBEV achieves more than an order of magnitude lower peak GPU memory and significant inference-latency speedups, with memory effectively independent of the number of height bins, reducing the operator's peak memory to O(BCXY) (output only). This unlocks higher BEV range/resolution and vertical discretization within fixed deployment budgets on memory-constrained devices. Our contribution is an execution redesign -- same math, different execution -- that removes a key scalability barrier for deployment-ready Sampling-VT. Code available at https://github.com/yokosyun/FlashBEV

</details>


### [3] [REVA-PO: Stabilizing Reinforcement Learning for Chest X-ray Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10147)
*Li Guo,Anas M. Tahir,Z. Jane Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出REVA-PO框架用于自动化胸部X光报告生成，通过响应权重正则化和验证锚定策略重置来稳定强化学习训练，在多项指标上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在胸部X光报告生成中受限于固定的KL正则化和静态参考策略，导致训练不稳定和探索受限。

Method: REVA-PO框架包含Response-Weighted Regularization（RER）和Validation-Anchored Policy Reset（VAPR）。RER根据优势和熵动态调整KL权重；VAPR定期将参考策略同步到最佳验证检查点。采用三阶段管道：预热训练、分类器引导的监督微调和RL。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR和IU-Xray上设置新SOTA：BLEU-4提升5.1%和3.6%，CheXpert F1提升4.5%，RadGraph F1提升12.8%。

Conclusion: REVA-PO显著提升了报告生成的语言质量和临床准确性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Automated chest X-ray report generation has recently benefited from reinforcement learning (RL) and large language models. However, RL training often suffers from instability or limited exploration due to fixed Kullback-Leibler (KL) regularization and a static reference policy that accumulates KL pressure over time. We propose Response-Weighted and Validation-Anchored Policy Optimization (REVA-PO), a RL framework that stabilizes long-term training via Response-Weighted Regularization (RER) and Validation-Anchored Policy Reset (VAPR). RER dynamically adjusts per-response KL weights based on advantage and reference-policy entropy, relaxing constraints for high-quality responses while tightening them for low-quality ones. Complementarily, VAPR periodically synchronizes the reference and current policies to the best validation checkpoint, resetting accumulated regularization pressure to expand the viable exploration space. To ensure a robust starting point, we employ a three-stage pipeline consisting of warm-up training, classifier-guided supervised fine-tuning, and RL. Extensive evaluations on MIMIC-CXR and IU-Xray demonstrate that REVA-PO sets new state-of-the-art benchmarks in both linguistic quality and clinical accuracy. Notably, BLEU-4 improves by 5.1% on MIMIC-CXR and 3.6% on IU-Xray, while CheXpert F1 and RadGraph F1 scores increase by 4.5% and 12.8%, respectively, over prior leading methods. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/LiGuo12/REVA_PO/.

</details>


### [4] [EmoStyle: Affective Conditioning of Style-Specialist Experts for Emotional Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10165)
*Dexiang Hong,Yijie Guo,Weidong Chen,Xinyan Liu,Zixuan Zou,Zhendong Mao,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EmoStyle是一个基于Z-Image的框架，通过LLM预测情感线索（效价-唤醒度、主导情感）并编码为条件向量，结合风格专用的LoRA适配器实现情感感知的艺术图像生成，在AffectiveArt Challenge 2026 Track 1中获得第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感感知艺术图像生成面临的主要挑战是训练数据中的视觉和情感属性在测试时不可用。生成器需要决定不仅画什么，还要通过颜色、光线、笔触、构图、线条和布局来表达目标情感，这导致了测试提示和精细控制条件之间的控制缺口。

Method: 1) Z-Image-based框架将输入提示转换为结构化生成状态；2) LLM推理器预测情感线索（效价-唤醒度、主导情感、治疗效应标签）和宽高比决策；3) 将情感字段编码为情感条件向量，通过AdaLN风格调制注入到去噪块中；4) 针对每种艺术风格训练专用的LoRA适配器，推理时选择相应专家；5) 轻量级VLM引导的候选选择步骤，根据提示对齐、风格一致性、情感表达和视觉质量对生成的图像进行排序。

Result: 在AffectiveArt Challenge 2026 Track 1中，USTC_PI_LAB_TEAM提交的作品获得第一名。

Conclusion: 通过将情感线索编码为直接引导中间特征生成的条件向量，并结合风格专用的LoRA适配器，EmoStyle有效解决了测试提示与精细控制条件之间的控制缺口问题，实现了高质量的情感感知艺术图像生成。

Abstract: Emotion-aware artistic image generation requires an image to match the input prompt, follow the specified artistic style, and convey the target emotion. In this challenge, the main difficulty is that the visual and affective attributes available in the training data are not explicitly provided at test time. Without these attributes, the generator has to decide not only what to depict, but also how the target emotion should be expressed through color, lighting, brushwork, composition, line, and layout. This creates a control gap between the available test prompt and the fine-grained conditions needed for emotion-aware artistic generation. To bridge this gap, we propose EmoStyle, a Z-Image-based framework that converts the input prompt into a structured generation state. An LLM reasoner first predicts affective cues (valence-arousal, dominant emotion, and therapeutic-effect labels) and an aspect-ratio decision. Instead of using these predictions only as additional prompt text, we encode the affective fields into an affective condition vector and inject it into the denoising blocks through AdaLN-style modulation. This allows the inferred control variables to directly guide the generation of intermediate features. Since emotional expression is also style-dependent, we further train a dedicated LoRA adapter for each artistic style bucket and select the corresponding expert during inference, enabling the same affective cues to be rendered with bucket-specific priors for color, texture, brushwork, and composition. Finally, a lightweight VLM-guided candidate selection step ranks the generated images based on prompt alignment, style consistency, emotional expression, and visual quality. In Track 1 of the AffectiveArt Challenge 2026, our USTC\_PI\_LAB\_TEAM submission achieved first place.

</details>


### [5] [Benchmarking Dynamic Affective Reasoning: A Viewer-Centric Video Emotion Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10238)
*Zhiyan Zhang,Peipei Song,Jinpeng Hu,Jingyang Jia,Xun Yang,Xiaojun Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了首个面向观众中心情感转变与因果推理的大规模视频情感分析基准DAR，包含15,087个视频和36,908个事件对齐的情感片段，涵盖27种情感类别。论文定义了情感分割、细粒度情感分类和情感推理三个任务，并提出DAR-R1框架（结合监督微调与Group Relative Policy Optimization），在10+多模态大模型上实现了动态情感推理的新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频情感分析将每个片段视为独立分类问题，忽略了情绪随连续因果事件累积反应而变化的心理学事实。论文旨在通过引入动态情感推理基准，从观众中心视角捕捉细粒度情感表达与转变，提供密集、时间锚定、因果明确的推理链。

Method: 提出Dynamic Affective Reasoning (DAR)基准数据集，包含15,087视频、36,908事件对齐情感片段、27情感类别。定义三个任务：情感分割、细粒度情感分类、情感推理。提出DAR-R1框架，采用两阶段方法：监督微调与Group Relative Policy Optimization。

Result: 在10+多模态大模型上评估，DAR-R1在动态情感推理任务上取得新SOTA，在情感定位和情感推理方面表现最佳。

Conclusion: DAR为视频情感分析提供了首个大规模观众中心情感转变与因果推理基准，DAR-R1框架有效提升了动态情感推理能力，为未来研究提供了新的任务定义和评估标准。

Abstract: Video emotion analysis is typically framed as a static classification problem, treating each clip as an independent labeled unit. However, such a formulation overlooks a key psychological fact: emotions change as a result of cumulative reactions to consecutive causal events. To bridge this gap, we introduce Dynamic Affective Reasoning, the first large-scale benchmark for viewer-centric affect transitions and causal reasoning over consecutive video events. DAR contains 15,087 videos and 36,908 event-aligned affective segments annotated with 27 emotion categories. Unlike existing video-based emotion datasets, DAR presents a new viewer-centric perspective on fine-grained emotional expressions and transitions, and provides dense, temporally grounded, and causally explicit reasoning chains. Based on DAR, we formally define three challenging tasks: affective segmentation, fine-grained emotion classification, and affective reasoning. Complementing this benchmark, we propose DAR-R1, a two-stage framework that combines supervised fine-tuning with Group Relative Policy Optimization. Experiments across 10+ MLLMs show that DAR-R1 sets a new state-of-the-art for dynamic affective reasoning, in terms of both emotional localization and affective reasoning. Project page: https://github.com/Zhang-Zhiyan/DAR.

</details>


### [6] [SynthDocBench: Controlled Benchmark for Long-Context Visual Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10400)
*Abhigya Verma,Khyati Mahajan,Amit Kumar Saha,Shruthan Radhakrishna,Sagar Davasam,Vikas Yadav,Sai Rajeswar Mudumba*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SynthDocBench合成基准测试，用于系统评估长上下文视觉文档理解模型，发现三种现有基准测试无法揭示的失败模式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs在现有基准测试上表现优异，但现实文档具有长度、布局复杂性、模态和问题难度等多种因素，现有基准测试难以控制这些变量，无法准确归因模型失败原因

Method: SynthDocBench采用组合设计，独立控制文档长度、布局结构、模态组成和问题类型；使用LLM流水线生成文档，覆盖六种布局原型，40%随机覆盖以防止模型利用虚假相关性；文档长度和结构多样性显著超过现有基准测试

Result: 评估七种前沿VLMs，发现三种失败模式：随文档长度急剧下降、系统性位置敏感性（中间三分之一最难）、长文档中图表理解失效；五分之六的模型出现负的早期-晚期趋势（最大下降8.3个百分点）

Conclusion: 当前模型可能过拟合于基准测试的人为特征，而非真正实现了鲁棒的长上下文视觉文档理解

Abstract: Vision language models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance on visual document understanding benchmarks such as DocVQA, ChartQA, and MMLongBench-Doc. However, real-world documents combine multiple factors such as length, layout complexity, modality, and question difficulty, which makes it difficult to attribute model failures to specific causes. We introduce SynthDocBench, a fully synthetic benchmark for long-context visual document understanding that systematically controls factors including document length, layout structure, modality composition, and question type. The benchmark is constructed using a combinatorial design, each factor is varied independently across generated documents, enabling controlled analysis of model behavior. Documents are generated end to end using an LLM pipeline across six layout archetypes, with a 40 percent random override to prevent models from exploiting spurious correlations. Additionally, SynthDocBench spans long-context documents with substantially greater length and structural diversity than existing benchmarks. Evaluating seven frontier VLMs, we uncover three failure modes that existing benchmarks cannot surface: sharp degradation with document length, a systematic positional sensitivity in which the middle third of a document is hardest for five of six models and five of six models show a negative Early-to-Late trend (steepest decline: 8.3 percentage points), and breakdown of chart comprehension in long-document settings. These results suggest that current models may be overfitting to benchmark artifacts rather than achieving robust long-context visual document understanding.

</details>


### [7] [Towards Autonomous and Auditable Medical Imaging Model Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10522)
*Shengyuan Liu,Jia-Xuan Jiang,Boyun Zheng,Cheng Wang,Zipei Wang,Wentao Pan,Hongtao Wu,Houwen Peng,Yu Gu,Lichao Sun,Yixuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AMID是一个用于医学影像模型开发的自主多智能体框架，通过数据条件方法规划和验证引导两阶段优化，在20个跨模态医学影像挑战任务中超越通用MLE系统，实现了从定制手工工程到智能体工作流的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在机器学习工程中展现自动化潜力，但医学影像任务面临模态特异性实验、严格验证协议和预测产物要求的挑战，难以直接将通用ML能力迁移到医学影像领域。

Method: AMID框架包含两大核心组件：1) 数据条件方法规划——将粗粒度任务级搜索空间细化为可执行、可并行的方法车道；2) 验证引导两阶段优化——从广泛探索转向选择性利用，全程严格验证验证协议、指标计算和预测产物。

Result: 在涵盖20个不同模态和预测类型的医学影像挑战任务中，AMID在多个任务上超越或匹敌强人类设计的挑战方案，证明了其在医学影像模型开发中的优越性。

Conclusion: AMID将医学影像模型开发从定制手工工程转变为智能体工作流，能够为异构任务生成高性能、可审计的模型产物，标志着医学影像开发进入自动化时代。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are beginning to automate machine learning engineering (MLE) by coupling planning, code execution, debugging, and empirical feedback. Translating this capability to medical imaging remains difficult because each task imposes modality-specific experimentation and strict requirements for validation protocols and prediction artifacts. Here we introduce AMID, an autonomous multi-agent framework for medical imaging model development. AMID first proposes Data-Conditioned Method Planning, which refines coarse task-level search spaces into executable, parallelizable method lanes grounded in task-specific data analysis and runnable medical-imaging resources. It then develops Verification-Guided Two-Stage Optimization, moving from broad early exploration of diverse method lanes to selective exploitation of promising candidates while enforcing strict verification of validation protocols, metric computation, and prediction artifacts throughout the optimization. Across 20 medical imaging challenge tasks spanning diverse modalities and prediction types, AMID outperformed evaluated general-purpose MLE systems and, on several tasks, approached or matched strong human-designed challenge solutions. These results suggest that AMID can turn task-specific medical imaging model development from bespoke manual engineering into an agentic workflow for producing high-performing and auditable model artifacts across heterogeneous tasks.

</details>


### [8] [WasteAssistant: Regulation-Guided Visual Question Answering Framework for Intelligent Waste Segregation and Sustainable Managemen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10610)
*Khush Kataruka,Harshit Maurya,Anuja Vats,Murari Mandal,Kiran Raja,Praveen Kumar Chandaliya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于视觉语言模型和大型多模态语言模型的智能垃圾分拣框架，构建了包含13,500个问答对的WasteVQA数据集，显著提升了垃圾分拣准确性和法规合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化垃圾分拣系统存在单模态视觉处理、上下文理解不足以及法规对齐性弱等局限性，无法满足可持续城市管理和环境治理的需求。

Method: 提出了语言引导的视觉AI框架，集成视觉语言模型和多模态大语言模型实现联合视觉-语言推理；构建了覆盖21类垃圾的WasteVQA数据集（13,500个问答对）；采用与印度2016年固体废物管理规则对齐的视觉问答范式；实验使用基于BLIP的模型。

Result: BLIP模型在BLEU和BERTScore上分别达到0.8291和0.9273，显著优于传统CNN方法；提高了源头级分拣准确性和法规合规性。

Conclusion: 该工作支持市政和公民端垃圾管理的可扩展部署，促进了多模态AI在可持续城市基础设施中的应用，开源代码和数据集已发布。

Abstract: Efficient waste segregation is critical for sustainable urban management and environmental governance. Existing automated systems are limited by single-modality visual processing, insufficient contextual understanding, and weak regulatory alignment. To address these issues, we propose a language-guided vision-AI framework that integrates vision-language models and multimodal large language models for joint visual-linguistic reasoning. This framework implements a visual question answering paradigm aligned with India's Solid Waste Management Rules 2016. We construct a new WasteVQA dataset with 13,500 question-answer pairs across 21 waste categories. Experiments show that the BLIP-based model achieves a BLEU score of 0.8291 and a BERTScore of 0.9273, outperforming traditional CNN-based methods. This work improves source-level segregation accuracy, ensures regulatory compliance, and supports scalable deployment for municipal and citizen-facing waste management, promoting multimodal AI in sustainable urban infrastructure. The source code and dataset are available at: https://github.com/Khushkataruka/WasteAssistant

</details>


### [9] [Traj-VLN: Learning Pixel-Space Interaction via Autoregressive Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10744)
*Changfei Fu,Guangcheng Chen,Wenjun Xu,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过微调视觉语言模型在2D像素空间进行自回归轨迹生成，直接预测像素坐标而非离散动作，从而提升连续环境下的视觉语言导航（VLN-CE）性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言导航需要智能体按照自然语言指令在未知环境中导航，可分解为一系列3D空间交互子任务。现有视觉语言模型主要在RGB图像上训练，缺乏深度和3D几何信息，导致对"沿深度方向移动"等空间交互难以理解。

Method: 提出微调VLM直接在2D像素空间学习导航交互，通过自回归轨迹生成方式，给定语言指令和历史观测，从当前观测底部中心开始依次预测一系列像素坐标，绘制轨迹路径。采用像素级轨迹监督而非离散动作学习。

Result: 像素空间轨迹监督显著提升VLN性能，旗舰模型在有限计算资源和训练数据下实现了最先进的性能，优于以往基于离散动作或依赖深度信息的方法。

Conclusion: 微调VLM进行2D像素轨迹生成是一种有效的VLN-CE方法，能在不依赖深度信息的情况下实现高性能导航，且计算成本较低。

Abstract: Benefiting from the powerful priors embedded in large-scale pre-training data and the emerging commonsense reasoning ability, large language models (LLMs) have shown unprecedented generalization capabilities in many research fields. Recently, projecting visual embeddings into the language space via vision-language models (VLMs) to achieve sim-toreal and cross-scene generalization has become a prevailing paradigm in the field of Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE). VLN requires an embodied agent to navigate through unseen environments following natural linguistic instructions. We emphasize that a VLN task can be decomposed into a sequence of sub-tasks, each corresponding to a process of 3D spatial interaction with the environments described by instructions such as "walk to the end of the sofa and turn left." However, such spatial interactions involving moving into the image along the direction of depth sensing are puzzling for VLMs as they were predominantly trained on conversations with RGB images. Rather than incorporating depth or 3D geometric information-which VLMs rarely encounter during pretrainingwe propose an alternative approach: fine-tuning VLMs to learn navigation interactions directly in 2D pixel space through autoregressive trajectory generation. Given a linguistic instruction and historical observations, our model sequentially predicts a series of pixel coordinates, drawing a trajectory from the bottom center of the current observation. While prior work has proved that pixel-goal supervision outperforms learning of discrete actions, our experiments further verify that the supervision of pixel-space trajectory significantly enhances VLN performance. Moreover, we demonstrate that our flagship model achieves state-of-the-art level performance with relatively limited computational resources and training data.

</details>


### [10] [Beyond the Eye: Efficient Multimodal Reasoning via Self-Regulated Implicit Visual Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11106)
*Xiuwei Chen,Quanlin Chen,Wentao Hu,Zisheng Chen,Kun Xiang,Zehua Ma,Mingyang Zhang,Jianhua Han,Hanhui Li,Hang Xu,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BEE（Beyond the Eye）隐式视觉工具范式，通过将视觉工具调用行为融入训练目标，使模型自适应平衡内部知识与隐式工具，避免冗余工具使用并显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"Thinking with Images"范式依赖频繁外部工具调用和重复图像编码，导致计算开销大、推理延迟高，限制了细粒度视觉感知任务的效率。

Method: BEE包含两阶段训练：(1) 正式化CoT监督微调SFT，构建带有结构化工具槽和混合调用状态的CoT轨迹，激活模型隐式工具表示和自适应切换能力；(2) 自调节奖励驱动对齐，引入Net Tool Gain（NTG）指标量化冗余工具使用，设计自调节奖励机制惩罚无效工具依赖并鼓励知识路由，确保仅在内知识不足时调用隐式工具。

Result: BEE在细粒度视觉感知任务上达到最先进性能，在通用推理任务上保持竞争力，推理效率显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过将工具调用行为直接融入训练目标并鼓励模型发展自我调节机制，BEE成功实现了内部知识与隐式工具的自适应平衡，有效避免了冗余工具使用，在保证性能的同时大幅降低了推理延迟。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made remarkable progress on fine-grained perception tasks under the "Thinking with Images" (TwI) paradigm by iteratively performing various visual tool operations. However, this paradigm relies heavily on frequent external tool calls and repeated image re-encoding, which leads to substantial computational overhead and inference latency. To address these issues, we propose Beyond the Eye (BEE), a novel implicit visual tool paradigm centered on self-regulated capability. BEE directly incorporates visual tool invocation behaviors into the training objective and encourages the model to develop a self-regulated invocation mechanism. This design enables the model to adaptively balance internal knowledge and implicit tools, avoiding redundant tool usage while substantially reducing inference latency. Specifically, BEE involves a two-stage training process: (1) Formalized Chain-of-Thought (CoT) Supervised Fine-tuning (SFT). We construct CoT trajectories with structured tool slots and mixed invocation states. This stage activates the model's implicit tool representations and adaptive switching capability. (2) Self-regulated Reward-Driven Alignment. To address redundant tool usage caused by ambiguous cognitive boundaries, we first introduce the Net Tool Gain (NTG) metric to quantify this phenomenon. Based on this observation, we further propose a self-regulated reward mechanism. This mechanism penalizes ineffective tool dependency and encourages the model to perform knowledge routing, ensuring that implicit tools are invoked only when the model's internal knowledge is insufficient. BEE achieves state-of-the-art performance in fine-grained visual perception while remaining competitive in general reasoning tasks and achieving substantial gains in inference efficiency.

</details>


### [11] [SLVMBench: Skill Learning from Video Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11312)
*Yudong Yang,Guangzhi Sun,Yixuan Li,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首个联合评估视频LLM从长视频记忆中学习技能并应用于实时任务的基准测试SLVMBench


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频LLM评估侧重被动理解或短技能学习，缺乏对完整记忆-提取-迁移流程的测试

Method: 构建2-3小时视频流，嵌入教程视频，要求模型回答实时问题；包含亚秒级时间校准、人工标注问题和无常识猜测的题目

Result: 现有视频LLM在从视频中学习和应用技能方面表现很差；当技能知识位于长视频记忆时性能显著下降

Conclusion: 揭示了现有视频LLM的关键局限，SLVMBench是首个研究长上下文视频记忆中实时技能获取和应用的基准

Abstract: We introduce Skill Learning from Video Memory (SLVMBench), the first benchmark that jointly evaluates whether video large language models (video-LLMs) can learn skills from long video memory and apply them to real-time tasks. SLVMBench presents models with 2-3 hour video streams that contain a tutorial video embedded in a stream of arbitrary irrelevant videos, resembling real-world human learning practices. Video-LLMs are asked to apply the acquired skill to answer real-time questions about an ongoing video. Unlike long-video understanding benchmarks that emphasize passive comprehension and skill-learning benchmarks that rely on short, immediate demonstrations, SLVMBench tests the full pipeline of memorizing and extracting procedural knowledge, as well as transferring it to real-time tasks. Moreover, rigorous human annotations feature sub-second-level temporal calibration, manually engineered questions eliminating common-sense guessing, and collated tutorials to ensure coverage of the required skills. Evaluations on state-of-the-art proprietary and open-source video LLMs show that video-LLMs struggle substantially with learning and applying skill knowledge from videos. Moreover, performance degrades markedly when the skill knowledge is placed within a long video memory. These results reveal a key limitation of existing video LLMs and position SLVMBench as the first benchmark for studying real-time skill acquisition and application from long-context video memory.

</details>


### [12] [HierCAD: Hierarchical Text-to-CAD Design via Structure Alignment and Parameter Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11339)
*Jimin Xu,Tianbao Wang,Tao Jin,Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HierCAD分层文本到CAD生成框架，通过渐进推理和结构对齐参数定位策略，在CAD序列生成和模型重建评估中超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的文本到CAD方法在复杂设计结构一致性和几何参数准确定位方面存在困难，难以有效处理CAD构建树中的层级推理问题。

Method: 1. 将CAD生成重新表述为渐进推理，将CAD构建树分解为对象级程序推理和零件级拓扑推理轨迹；2. 提出统一的结构对齐与参数定位（SAPG）学习策略：结构对齐将拓扑推理轨迹与相应的参数化CAD跨度对齐，参数定位通过结构保持的参数扰动和基于排名的监督缓解捷径学习。

Result: 在CAD序列生成和重建CAD模型评估上均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: HierCAD通过分层推理框架和结构对齐参数定位策略有效解决了文本到CAD生成中的结构一致性和参数定位问题，为复杂CAD设计生成提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent text-to-CAD approaches have shown promising results by leveraging large language models, but they often struggle with maintaining structural consistency in complex designs and accurately grounding geometric parameters. To address these issues, we propose HierCAD, a hierarchical text-to-CAD framework that improves both structural reasoning and parameter prediction. HierCAD reformulates CAD generation as progressive reasoning by decomposing CAD construction trees into object-level procedural reasoning and part-level topology reasoning trajectories. To further improve generation fidelity, we introduce a unified Structure Alignment and Parameter Grounding (SAPG) learning strategy. Structure alignment aligns topology reasoning trajectories with their corresponding parametric CAD spans, while parameter grounding mitigates shortcut learning through structure-preserving parameter perturbations and ranking-based supervision. Experiments demonstrate that HierCAD outperforms prior state-of-the-art methods on both CAD sequence generation and reconstructed CAD model evaluation. Our code is available at https://github.com/Collab-Gen/HierCAD.

</details>


### [13] [Parse, Search, and Confirmation: Training-Free Aerial Vision-and-Dialog Navigation with Chain-of-Thought Reasoning and Structured Spatial Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11529)
*Yu Qi,Hongyu Li,Shaofei Huang,Tianrui Hui,Yaxiong Wang,Lechao Cheng,Zhun Zhong,Si Liu,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种无需训练的PSC-AVDN框架，通过将解析-搜索-确认推理流程与结构化空间记忆(SSM)紧密结合，解决了高海拔无人机导航中对话指令的方向性定位困难与空间记忆缺失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接应用多模态大语言模型(MLLMs)进行AVDN任务时存在两个关键问题：1) 方向性定位能力弱，导致导航不可靠；2) 缺乏显式的空间记忆机制，难以在长距离导航中保持一致性。传统训练方法计算成本高，因此需要在无需训练的设置下实现高效导航。

Method: PSC-AVDN框架包含三个核心组件：1) 解析阶段，利用LLM将模糊的对话指令转换为稳定的几何方向和目的地提示；2) 搜索链式思维(S-CoT)和确认链式思维(C-CoT)，分别在高空观察下执行逐步目标探索和候选区域精细验证；3) 结构化空间记忆(SSM)整合三种互补的空间线索源（多尺度视觉观测、空间视觉记忆、结构几何记忆），提供全局空间背景和长距离一致性。

Result: 在ANDH和ANDH-Full数据集上的广泛实验表明，PSC-AVDN在无需训练的设置下建立了新的最先进性能，其结果与多个经过微调的方法相匹配甚至超越。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了无需训练的框架结合结构化空间记忆可以有效解决航空视觉对话导航中的关键挑战，为资源高效的无人机导航提供了一种实用且高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we tackle the Aerial Vision-and-Dialog Navigation (AVDN) task in the training-free setting for resource-efficient high-altitude UAV navigation.Naively applying MLLMs leads to unreliable navigation due to weak directional grounding and the lack of explicit spatial memory.To address these issues, we propose PSC-AVDN, a training-free framework that tightly couples a three-stage Parsing-Search-Confirmation reasoning pipeline with a Structured Spatial Memory (SSM).The parsing stage uses an LLM to convert ambiguous dialogue instructions into stable geometric directional and destination cues.A Search Chain-of-Thought (S-CoT) then performs stepwise target exploration under high-altitude observations, and a Confirmation Chain-of-Thought (C-CoT) conducts fine-grained verification around candidate regions to resolve visual ambiguity.Meanwhile, SSM integrates three complementary sources of spatial cues, including multi-scale visual observation, spatial visual memory, and structured geometric memory to provide global spatial context and long-horizon consistency.Extensive experiments on ANDH and ANDH-Full show that PSC-AVDN establishes new state-of-the-art performance in the training-free setting, matching or surpassing several finetuned methods.Code will be publicly available at: https://github.com/QY6616/PSC-AVDN

</details>


### [14] [MonkeyOCRv2: A Visual-Text Foundation Model for Document AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11562)
*Yuliang Liu,Zhang Li,Ziyang Zhang,Shuo Zhang,Qiang Liu,Jiajun Song,Zidun Guo,Xinhan Wang,Handong Zheng,Yang Liu,Dongliang Luo,Zhiyin Ma,Jiarui Zhang,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MonkeyOCRv2是一种面向文档AI的视觉-文本预训练模型，通过构建包含1.13亿图像、17种语言的MonkeyDoc v2数据集，采用图像-文本生成与像素级文档重建的联合预训练策略，在5项文档分析任务中均显著提升性能，作为多模态大语言模型的视觉编码器时，可构建0.7B文档解析模型，在MDPBench基准上超越3B dots.mocr 2.8%绝对性能，且视觉编码器规模仅为其1/11。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流视觉编码器在自然图像上预训练，难以有效应用于文档图像；文档图像具有密集文本和细粒度字符笔画，需要字符级视觉感知能力。

Method: 1. 构建MonkeyDoc v2数据集（1.13亿图像，17种语言）；2. 提出联合预训练策略，同时进行图像-文本生成（对齐视觉表示与文本内容）和像素级文档重建（保留字符笔画和布局细节）。

Result: 在文本识别、公式识别、文本检测、文档篡改检测、重叠文本分割5项任务中，替换原始编码器均提升性能；作为多模态大语言模型的视觉编码器，在MDPBench上超越3B dots.mocr 2.8%绝对性能，视觉编码器规模仅为其1/11；在8个基准测试中，文档理解性能优于CLIP、DINO、SAM。

Conclusion: 面向文档的视觉预训练可以作为文档智能的基础，MonkeyOCRv2证明了文档专用视觉编码器的有效性，在保持模型更小的同时实现了更优性能。

Abstract: Mainstream visual encoders are pretrained on natural images and cannot be effectively applied to document images without document-oriented adaptation, as dense text and fine-grained character strokes demand character-level visual perception. We present MonkeyOCRv2, a visual-text pretrained model for document AI. First, we construct MonkeyDoc v2, to our knowledge the largest document-image pretraining corpus, comprising 113 million images spanning 17 languages. Second, we propose a pretraining strategy that jointly learns image-to-text generation and pixel-level document reconstruction: the former aligns visual representations with textual content, while the latter preserves character strokes and layout details. Extensive experiments are conducted on five representative document analysis tasks, including text recognition, formula recognition, text detection, document tampering detection, and overlapping text segmentation. Replacing the original encoders with MonkeyOCRv2 consistently improves performance across all five tasks. Finally, we validate its effectiveness as the vision encoder of multimodal large language models on the more challenging tasks of document parsing and document understanding. Kept frozen and paired with a lightweight language model, it yields a 0.7B document parsing model that sets a new open-source state-of-the-art on MDPBench, a recent benchmark spanning digital-born and photographed documents across 17 languages, surpassing the previous best 3B dots.mocr by 2.8% absolute with a vision encoder roughly 11$\times$ smaller. The frozen encoder also powers a document understanding model that outperforms counterparts built on CLIP, DINO, and SAM across eight benchmarks under identical training settings. These results suggest that document-oriented visual pretraining can serve as a foundation for document intelligence in its own right.

</details>


### [15] [Beyond the Single Camera: Agentic Multi-View Reasoning in Sports Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11844)
*Kerui Chen,Jinglu Wang,Xiaoyi Zhang,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SportMV-Bench基准和SportMV-Agent框架，用于评估多视角体育视频理解能力。SportMV-Bench包含787个视频片段和2592个问答对，涵盖感知识别、规则事件解释和裁决决策推理三个类别。实验表明当前MLLMs难以有效利用多视角信息，瓶颈在于细粒度视觉感知和视角选择而非逻辑推理。SportMV-Agent通过主动视角选择、感知工具执行和证据推理的迭代循环，相比最强基线模型提升14.46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在单视角视频理解上表现优异，但体育视频存在密集遮挡、快速运动和复杂交互，难以从单一视角解决。体育比赛通常从多个角度录制，提供互补证据供裁判使用，但缺乏评估MLLMs多视角体育视频理解能力的基准。

Method: 1. 构建SportMV-Bench：从官方比赛录像构建，采用LLM生成、MLLM验证和人工过滤的流水线，包含787个多视角视频片段和2592个问答对，分为感知识别(PAR)、规则事件解释(REI)和裁决决策推理(ADR)三类。2. 提出SportMV-Agent：主动视角选择、感知工具执行和证据推理的迭代循环框架。

Result: 当前MLLMs难以有效利用多视角信息，瓶颈在于细粒度视觉感知和视角选择而非逻辑推理或领域知识。SportMV-Agent实现了14.46%的相对性能提升，证明了主动视角选择和感知工具的重要性。

Conclusion: SportMV-Bench为多视角体育视频理解提供了全面基准，SportMV-Agent通过聚焦视角选择和细粒度感知解决了现有MLLMs的局限性，展示了代理框架在利用多视角信息方面的有效性。

Abstract: Recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance on single-view video understanding benchmarks. However, sports videos involve dense occlusion, rapid motion, and complex interactions that are difficult to resolve from a single viewpoint. In practice, sports events are recorded from multiple camera angles, providing complementary evidence used by referees. Yet, no existing benchmark evaluates MLLMs on multi-view sports video understanding. To address this gap, we introduce SportMV-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark built from official match recordings, through a dedicated pipeline combining LLM-based generation, MLLM-based verification, and human filtering to ensure quality and consistency. SportMV-Bench containing 787 multi-view video bundles and 2592 question-answer pairs across three categories: Perception-Aware Recognition (PAR), Rule-aware Event Interpretation (REI), and Adjudicative Decision Reasoning(ADR). Our analysis shows that current MLLMs fail to effectively exploit multi-view information, with the bottlenecks lying in fine-grained visual perception and view selection rather than logical reasoning or domain knowledge. We propose SportMV-Agent, an agentic framework that orchestrates an iterative loop of active view selection, perception tool execution, and evidence-grounded reasoning, achieving a significant 14.46% relative improvement over the strongest MLLM baseline.

</details>


### [16] [Evidence-Backed Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11862)
*Shijie Wang,Honglu Zhou,Ziyang Wang,Ran Xu,Caiming Xiong,Silvio Savarese,Chen Sun,Juan Carlos Niebles*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出证据支持的视频问答（E-VQA）任务，要求模型同时输出语义答案和精确的时空证据（时间片段及目标分割掩码），并构建了ST-Evidence基准和ST-Evidence-Instruct数据集，揭示QA准确性与视觉感知之间的解耦问题，通过在数据集上微调模型显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频大语言模型作为黑盒提供文本答案但缺乏可验证的视觉依据，现有解释性方法依赖稀疏边界框或文本理由，难以捕捉遮挡和非刚性形变等复杂视频动态。

Method: 提出E-VQA任务，要求模型联合输出语义答案和时空证据；引入ST-Evidence基准（人类验证，包含判别式和生成式像素级定位）；创建ST-Evidence-Instruct数据集（16万规模，通过自动化生成管道连接高层推理与细粒度定位）。

Result: 评估表明当前模型存在QA准确性与真实视觉感知之间的关键解耦，单纯缩放无法弥合；在ST-Evidence-Instruct上微调的模型在7B模型上相比匹配大小的UniPixel基线分别提升27.2 t-mean和13.8 J&F。

Conclusion: 建立了解释性、证据支持的视频理解基线，提供了新的基准和大规模数据集，推动视频大语言模型从黑盒向可解释、可验证方向发展。

Abstract: Current Video Large Language Models (Video LLMs) excel in question answering (QA) but largely operate as black boxes, providing textual answers without verifiable visual grounding. Existing explainability efforts rely on textual rationales or sparse bounding boxes, which struggle to capture complex video dynamics such as occlusions and non-rigid deformations. We propose Evidence-Backed Video Question Answering (E-VQA), a novel task requiring models to jointly output a semantic answer and precise spatio-temporal evidence: temporal segments and dense, tracked object segmentation masklets. To support this, we introduce ST-Evidence, the first human-verified benchmark for both discriminative and generative pixel-level grounding. Evaluations of state-of-the-art models reveal a critical decoupling between QA accuracy and true visual perception that scaling alone fails to bridge. To address this, we develop scalable, automated generation pipelines to create ST-Evidence-Instruct, a 160k-scale dataset bridging high-level reasoning with fine-grained grounding. Fine-tuning grounded Video LLMs on this data yields substantial gains over the corresponding size-matched UniPixel baselines (e.g., +27.2 t-mean and +13.8 J&F on a 7B model), establishing a robust baseline for explainable, evidence-backed video understanding. Code and data are available at https://github.com/SalesforceAIResearch/EVQA.

</details>


### [17] [Read It Back: Pretrained MLLMs Are Zero-Shot Reward Models for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11886)
*Runhui Huang,Qihui Zhang,Zhe Liu,Yu Gao,Jie Wu,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpectraReward 是一种训练奖励函数，通过图像条件的前向传播测量提示恢复程度，将预训练 MLLM 转换为图像生成强化学习的奖励模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 MLLM 衍生奖励模型需要偏好标签和奖励模型微调，作者希望提出一种无需训练的奖励函数，能直接复用 MLLM 的预训练图像-文本对齐能力。

Method: SpectraReward 测量原始提示可以从生成图像中恢复的程度，使用平均图像条件提示对数似然作为奖励。Self-SpectraReward 是特殊情况，其中策略自身的理解分支作为生成分支的奖励模型，形成闭环自改进框架。

Result: 在两个扩散模型、三种 RL 算法、九个奖励 MLLM 骨干模型（4B-235B 参数）和五个分布外基准上验证。SpectraReward 和 Self-SpectraReward 显著提高生成性能，优于之前方法；Self-SpectraReward 可匹配或超越更大的外部奖励模型。

Conclusion: 奖励-策略对齐是有效图像生成 RL 的关键因素。更大的奖励 MLLM 不总是更好，自给式奖励模型可以超越更大的外部奖励模型。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose SpectraReward, a training-free reward function that turns pretrained MLLMs into off-the-shelf reward models for image-generation reinforcement learning. Instead of asking the MLLM to judge a generated image or answer decomposed verification questions, SpectraReward measures how well the original prompt can be recovered from the generated image through a single image-conditioned, teacher-forced forward pass. We use the average image-conditioned prompt log-likelihood as the reward, directly reusing the MLLM's pretrained image-text alignment ability without preference labels, reward-model fine-tuning. We further introduce Self-SpectraReward, a special case for unified multimodal models where the policy's own understanding branch serves as the reward model for its generation branch, forming a closed-loop self-improving framework without external reward models or external knowledge. Extensive experiments validate SpectraReward through a broad image-generation RL study covering two diffusion models, three RL algorithms, nine reward MLLM backbones from four MLLM families spanning 4B to 235B parameters, and five out-of-distribution text-to-image benchmarks. Results show that both SpectraReward and Self-SpectraReward significantly and consistently improve generation performance and outperform prior MLLM-derived reward training methods. Further analysis reveals that larger reward MLLMs are not always better, while Self-SpectraReward can match or surpass much larger external reward models, suggesting that reward-policy alignment is a key factor for effective image-generation RL. Project Page: https://huangrh99.github.io/SpectraReward/

</details>

### [18] [Global Merger-Arbitrage Forecasting with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09921)
*Hinal Jajal,Michal Mucha,Charles Sweat,Chris Pulman,Charlie Flanagan,Peter Anderson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于语言模型的并购套利预测系统，通过长上下文推理和专家引导的上下文工程，在400多起大型跨国并购交易中实现了优于市场隐含概率、XGBoost和前沿语言模型的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与以往专注于混合话题基准和短上下文新闻片段的LLM判断性预测研究不同，本文聚焦于需要处理数百页技术文档的长上下文推理的并购套利这一高风险金融场景。

Method: 系统结合了专家引导的上下文工程和基于历史交易事后指导推理的微调。给定已宣布的交易，系统输出三种互斥结果的概率分布：按公告条款成交、更高出价或交易终止。

Result: 在400多起大型跨国并购交易的样本外测试中，该系统将类别平衡的Brier分数降低至0.151，比市场隐含概率低24%，比XGBoost低19%，比前沿语言模型低25-42%。

Conclusion: 结合事后监督和专家设计的上下文，LLM能够在专业、长上下文的金融工作流程中取得成功，证明了语言模型在特定金融领域的预测能力。

Abstract: We present a language-model forecasting system for merger arbitrage, a specialized high-stakes financial setting in which the task is to predict the outcome of announced M\&A deals. Unlike prior work on judgmental forecasting with LLMs, which has focused on broad mixed-topic benchmarks and short context such as news snippets, we study a setting that requires long-context reasoning over hundreds of pages of technical documents. Our system combines expert-guided context engineering with finetuning on hindsight-guided reasoning traces derived from historical deals. Given an announced deal, it outputs a probability distribution over three mutually exclusive outcomes: closing at announced terms, a higher bid, or deal termination. On an out-of-sample set of more than 400 large deals spanning 42 countries, our finetuned system achieves the best performance of any method we evaluate, reducing class-balanced Brier score to 0.151. This is 24\% below calibrated market-implied probabilities, 19\% below XGBoost, and 25-42\% below frontier language models. These results, together with ablation studies, show that LLM-based forecasting can succeed in specialized, long-context financial workflows, with hindsight-based supervision and expert-designed context playing a critical role.

</details>

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to summarize clinical trial results for healthcare providers, patients, and payers, but their tendency to hallucinate poses significant risks in this high-stakes context. This study introduces a benchmark evaluation framework for measuring the faithfulness of LLM-generated clinical trial summaries across three stakeholder audiences. The framework consists of 200 stratified trials drawn from the Aggregate Analysis of ClinicalTrials.gov database, evaluated using audience-specific prompt templates and a six-dimension faithfulness annotation schema. Baseline measurements were established for GPT-4o, Claude Sonnet 4.6, and Gemini 2.5 Flash across 1,800 generated summaries scored using a cross-encoder natural language inference (NLI) model. Unsupported Claims was identified as the dominant failure mode across all three models, with a mean annotation score of 1.55 out of three. A knowledge-graph-augmented retrieval system was developed and evaluated against the baseline, producing statistically significant improvements in NLI-based faithfulness scores (entailment +0.0125, faithfulness +0.0130, p < 0.0001). Improvement pathways were model-dependent, with GPT-4o improving primarily through contradiction reduction while Claude Sonnet 4.6 and Gemini 2.5 Flash improved through increased entailment.

</details>


### [20] [Silent Failures in Quantized LLM Reasoning: A Taxonomy-Based Analysis of Hollow Convergence and Failure Mode Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09999)
*Renuka Oladri,Mohan Vamsi Varadaraju Priya,Jerry Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现量化技术会改变大语言模型的推理方式，即使任务准确率保持不变。通过对30000个思维链输出的分类分析，发现在NF4量化下，模型会出现"Hollow Convergence"（空洞收敛）推理失败模式，且这种变化无法通过表面文本特征检测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化技术广泛应用于大语言模型部署，但量化是否会影响模型的推理方式而不仅仅是准确率，这一问题尚未得到充分研究。研究动机在于探索量化是否会引入"静默"的推理模式变化，这种变化可能无法通过传统准确率指标检测到。

Method: 使用六类失败模式分类体系（经两名独立人类标注员验证，Cohen's κ=0.906），对5个指令微调大语言模型（3B-14B参数）在FP32、FP16和NF4三种量化精度下的30000个思维链输出进行分类分析，涵盖四个推理基准测试。还分析了表面文本特征与推理失败模式的关联性。

Result: 准确率在量化前后保持稳定（最大仅下降3.1个百分点）。但推理模式发生显著变化：NF4量化下，"空洞收敛"（Hollow Convergence）在较小模型中急剧下降，但在12B及以上模型中保持不变。该效应因基准测试而异：GSM8K免疫，LogiQA和ARC-Challenge变化最大。在LLaMA 3.2-3B中，捷径坍缩从44%上升到78%的错误答案失败，而信心雪崩从15.8%降至接近零。表面文本特征无法可靠检测空洞收敛（最佳F1=0.53）。

Conclusion: 量化技术会引入"空洞收敛"等推理失败模式，这是部署相关但无法通过标准评估管道检测到的失败模式。量化可以在保持准确率的同时改变模型的推理方式，这种变化对模型部署至关重要。

Abstract: We show that post-training quantization can silently alter how large language models reason even when task accuracy is preserved. Using a six-category failure taxonomy validated by two independent human annotators (Cohen's $κ$ = 0.906), we classify 30,000 chain-of-thought outputs from five instruction-tuned LLMs (3B--14B parameters) across three quantization precisions (FP32, FP16, NF4) and four reasoning benchmarks. We find that while accuracy is robust across precisions (maximum 3.1 pp drop), Hollow Convergence (correct answers reached through incomplete or unverifiable reasoning) shows a significant size-dependent shift under NF4, dropping sharply for the two smallest models tested but remaining invariant for models at 12B parameters and above. This effect is also benchmark-specific: GSM8K is categorically immune while LogiQA and ARC-Challenge show the largest shifts. Furthermore, under NF4, Shortcut Collapse rises from 44% to 78% of wrong-answer failures in LLaMA 3.2-3B while Confidence Snowballing collapses from 15.8% to near zero, a qualitative shift invisible to accuracy metrics. Finally, we show Hollow Convergence cannot be reliably detected from surface-level text features (best F1 = 0.53), establishing it as a deployment-relevant failure mode that standard evaluation pipelines cannot catch.

</details>


### [21] [Cost of Reasoning in non-English Languages: A Case Study on Japanese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10114)
*Yuu Jinnai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究训练一个在日语中推理的语言模型（Qwen-3-Swallow-8B的日语变体）的可行性，使用GRPO方法持续预训练，结果表明日语推理控制是可行的，但性能与强英语基线相当，且在文化相关任务上表现较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理轨迹对模型可解释性和安全性很重要，但RLMs在英语中表现最好，因为英语推理训练数据最丰富；因此需要研究如何开发能在用户选择语言（如日语）中推理的模型。

Method: 开发Qwen-3-Swallow-8B的日语推理变体（从Qwen-3-8B持续预训练），使用GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）训练方法，在编码、数学和科学基准测试以及日语文化基准测试上评估模型性能。

Result: 1）通过GRPO训练日语持续预训练模型，推理语言控制是可行的；2）在编码、数学和科学基准测试上，日语模型性能与强英语推理基线相当；3）在日语文化基准测试上表现比基线模型差。

Conclusion: 日语推理控制是可行的，但日语推理不会自动提高文化相关任务的表现；要获得与英语推理相当的性能，可能需要额外的优化策略。

Abstract: Reasoning Language Models (RLMs) achieve their strongest performance when they reason in English, the language for which reasoning-oriented training data is most abundant. However, reasoning trace is a clue for model interpretability and safety, and useful in practice for both the model users and for model developers. Thus, it is desirable to be able to develop a model that reasons in a language of the user's choice, while still maintaining strong reasoning performance. To this end, we study the feasibility of training a model that reasons in Japanese. We develop a Japanese-reasoning variant of Qwen-3-Swallow-8B, which is a Japanese LLM continually pretrained from Qwen-3-8B, with GRPO and evaluate it across coding, math, and science benchmarks. The study shows that reasoning-language control is feasible by training a Japanese continually pretrained model with GRPO. However, its performance is at best on par with strong English-reasoning baselines on several benchmarks. We also evaluate the trained model on Japanese cultural benchmarks and observe that the model's performance is worse than the baseline models, suggesting that the reasoning in Japanese does not immediately improve performance on culturally relevant tasks for free.

</details>


### [22] [Consensus vs. Dissent: Dynamic LLM Modeling of Subjective Preferences in Group Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10235)
*Cedric Waterschoot,Nava Tintarev,Francesco Barile*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过微调大语言模型（LLM）使其具备模拟人类群体判断的能力，用于在推荐系统中动态选择最优聚合策略。实验表明，LLM在用户研究中实现了最高的满意度和群体共识评分，特别是在考虑群体配置交互效应时与人类感知高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体推荐系统研究表明聚合策略的选择应考虑群体偏好分布，但先前研究未探索LLM是否能像人类一样根据群体配置（如少数派或联盟）感知公平、满意度和共识。本研究旨在验证LLM是否具备这种敏感性，以实现更符合人类主观判断的推荐。

Method: 1) 使用DeepSeek-V3.1推理数据集和人类真值评估微调LLM，构建Judgmental Llama和Judgmental OLMo作为实时判断模型；2) 基于社会选择聚合策略生成多个推荐候选；3) 动态选择最大化预测人类评估的方案；4) 进行包含284名用户的用户研究验证。

Result: 用户研究显示该方法在满意度和群体共识上获得最高评分；当考虑LLM方法与群体配置（如少数派或联盟）的交互效应时，LLM判断与人类感知在公平、满意度和共识三个维度上最一致。

Conclusion: 验证了动态适应聚合策略的必要性，证明了LLM能够实现与人类主观判断一致的推荐系统自适应，为群体推荐系统提供了基于LLM的新研究方向。

Abstract: Previous work in group recommender systems has demonstrated a sensitivity to the distribution of preferences within a group. Specifically, the selection of the preference aggregation strategy benefits from considering such group configurations. In this paper, we study whether LLMs are able to mimic this sensitivity and to select the ideal aggregation strategy (and corresponding recommendation) according to nuanced human perceptions of fairness, satisfaction, and consensus.
  We do this by fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs) on human survey data to serve as real-time judgmental models within the recommendation pipeline. Using a reasoning dataset distilled from DeepSeek-V3.1 and human ground truth assessments, we develop Judgmental Llama and Judgmental OLMo to simulate group assessments. Our pipeline successfully generates multiple recommendation candidates based on social choice-based aggregation strategies and dynamically selects the one that maximizes these predicted human-like evaluations. We further validate these suggestions in a user study (n=284) and find that our methodology achieved the highest scores for satisfaction and group consensus. Furthermore, we find that LLM judgments are most aligned with human perceptions of fairness, satisfaction and consensus when we also consider interaction effects between our LLM-based method and group configuration (e.g., minority or coalition). These findings give further support for dynamically adapting aggregation strategies to specific within-group preference distributions, and highlight the advantage of using LLMs for an adaptation that is aligned with subjective human judgments.

</details>


### [23] [PTEI: Integrating Personality Traits to Enhance Emotional Intelligence in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10245)
*Amir Reza Jafari,Praboda Rajapaksha,Reza Farahbakhsh,Noel Crespi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PTEI框架通过从情感场景中提取MBTI和OCEAN人格特质，结合对比学习和个性化提示增强LLM的情感理解能力，在多个基准测试中显著提升性能，与思维链结合后准确率额外提高4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管情感智能领域取得进展，LLM在复杂情感推理方面仍显著落后于人类。部分原因在于缺乏对个体差异（特别是人格特质）的充分整合，而人格特质是人类情感推理的基础。

Method: 1. 从情感场景中直接提取MBTI和OCEAN人格特质；2. 将人格特质作为上下文知识嵌入个性化提示中；3. 采用对比学习构建优化的检索系统，筛选情感和个人匹配的场景；4. 将PTEI与思维链推理结合。

Result: 在多个情感智能基准测试中，PTEI增强了各种LLM的情感理解能力，GPT模型改进最显著；与思维链结合后，准确率额外提升4%。

Conclusion: PTEI为开发具有更复杂社会和心理基础的AI系统做出了重要贡献，展示了整合人格特质对提升LLM情感推理能力的关键作用。

Abstract: Despite advances in Emotional Intelligence (EI), Large Language Models (LLMs) still significantly underperform humans in complex emotional reasoning. This gap originates partly from the limited incorporation of individual differences, particularly personality traits, which are fundamental to human emotional inference. To address this, we propose PTEI, a novel framework for integrating Personality Traits into Emotional Intelligence tasks using LLMs. In PTEI, MBTI and OCEAN personality traits are first extracted directly from the given emotional scenarios and then utilized as contextual knowledge within personality-aware prompts, guiding LLMs to accurately infer emotions and their underlying causes. To ensure optimal contextual grounding, we employ Contrastive Learning to construct an optimized retrieval system that surfaces emotionally and personally aligned scenarios, enhancing reasoning quality. Extensive experiments on established EI benchmarks show that PTEI enhances the Emotional Understanding (EU) capabilities of various LLMs, with the strongest improvement observed in GPT models. Combining PTEI with Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning yields an additional 4 percent increase in accuracy. These findings underscore PTEI's contribution toward advancing AI systems with more sophisticated social and psychological grounding.

</details>


### [24] [Structured Thoughts For Improved Reasoning And Context Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10386)
*Zain Sarwar,Supriyo Chakraborty,Berkcan Kapusuzoglu,Chia-Hsuan Lee,Anirban Das,Stephen Rawls,Kartik Balasubramaniam,Sambit Sahu*

Main category: cs.CL
